}); } } // --- Stage 1A: Revision History Functions --- function loadCodexRevisions(entityId) { var list = document.getElementById("codexRevisionList"); if (!list) return; list.innerHTML = "
Loading history...
"; callAppsScriptJson("listCodexRevisions", [entityId]).then(function (json) { var result = typeof json === 'string' ? JSON.parse(json) : json; if (!result || !result.ok || !result.revisions || !result.revisions.length) { list.innerHTML = "
No revision history yet.
"; return; } var entities = skCurrentSeriesId ? (codexCache.entitiesBySeries[skCurrentSeriesId] || []) : []; var currentEntity = entities.find(function (e) { return Number(e.id) === entityId; }) || null; renderCodexRevisionList(result.revisions, currentEntity); }).catch(function (err) { list.innerHTML = "
Failed to load history: " + escapeHtml(String(err.message || err)) + "
"; }); } function renderCodexRevisionList(revisions, currentEntity) { var list = document.getElementById("codexRevisionList"); if (!list) return; var html = ""; revisions.forEach(function (rev, idx) { var snapshot = rev.snapshot || {}; var date = rev.created_at ? new Date(rev.created_at).toLocaleString() : "Unknown"; var summary = rev.change_summary || "No summary"; var revNum = rev.revision_number || (idx + 1); var diffHtml = ""; // Compare each revision against the next older revision to show what changed var compareTo = null; if (idx < revisions.length - 1) { compareTo = revisions[idx + 1].snapshot || {}; } if (compareTo) { var diff = computeEntityFieldDiff(snapshot, compareTo); if (diff.length) { diffHtml = "
"; diff.forEach(function (d) { diffHtml += "

No revision history yet.
"; } } else { setStatus("Failed to delete revision.", "error"); } }).catch(function (err) { setStatus("Failed to delete revision: " + (err.message || err), "error"); }); }); }); } function computeEntityFieldDiff(oldSnapshot, newSnapshot) { var diffs = []; var fields = ["name", "description", "summary", "image_url", "color", "scope", "status", "visibility", "canon_status", "public_summary", "marketing_summary", "spoiler_level", "featured", "aliases"]; fields.forEach(function (f) { var oldVal = oldSnapshot[f]; var newVal = newSnapshot[f]; if (JSON.stringify(oldVal) !== JSON.stringify(newVal)) { diffs.push({ field: f, path: "", oldValue: oldVal, newValue: newVal }); } }); // Compare custom_data var oldCustom = oldSnapshot.custom_data || {}; var newCustom = newSnapshot.custom_data || {}; var customKeys = Object.keys(oldCustom).concat(Object.keys(newCustom)).filter(function (v, i, a) { return a.indexOf(v) === i; }); customKeys.forEach(function (k) { if (k === "sections") { var oldSections = oldCustom.sections || {}; var newSections = newCustom.sections || {}; var sectionKeys = Object.keys(oldSections).concat(Object.keys(newSections)).filter(function (v, i, a) { return a.indexOf(v) === i; }); sectionKeys.forEach(function (sk) { if (JSON.stringify(oldSections[sk]) !== JSON.stringify(newSections[sk])) { diffs.push({ field: "custom_data", path: "sections." + sk, oldValue: oldSections[sk], newValue: newSections[sk] }); } }); } else { if (JSON.stringify(oldCustom[k]) !== JSON.stringify(newCustom[k])) { diffs.push({ field: "custom_data", path: k, oldValue: oldCustom[k], newValue: newCustom[k] }); } } }); return diffs; } function saveManualCodexRevision() { if (!_codexActiveEntityId) { setStatus("No entity selected.", "error"); return; } var entities = skCurrentSeriesId ? (codexCache.entitiesBySeries[skCurrentSeriesId] || []) : []; var entity = entities.find(function (e) { return Number(e.id) === _codexActiveEntityId; }); if (!entity) { setStatus("No entity selected.", "error"); return; } var summary = prompt("Revision note (optional):", ""); if (summary === null) return; // user cancelled var snapshot = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(entity)); callAppsScriptJson("listCodexRevisions", [_codexActiveEntityId]).then(function (json) { var result = typeof json === 'string' ? JSON.parse(json) : json; if (result && result.ok) { setStatus("Revision restored successfully.", "ok"); // Reload the entity detail if (typeof openCodexEntityPage === "function") openCodexEntityPage(entityId); } else { setStatus("Failed to restore revision.", "error"); } }).catch(function (err) { setStatus("Failed to restore revision: " + (err.message || err), "error"); }); } // --- Revision Snapshot Modal --- function openRevisionSnapshotModal(rev, revIdx) { var modal = document.getElementById("revisionSnapshotModal"); if (!modal) return; var snapshot = rev.snapshot || {}; var date = rev.created_at ? new Date(rev.created_at).toLocaleString() : "Unknown"; var revNum = rev.revision_number || (revIdx + 1); // Populate header var title = document.getElementById("revisionSnapshotTitle"); if (title) title.textContent = "Revision " + revNum + " — " + (snapshot.name || "Untitled"); var meta = document.getElementById("revisionSnapshotMeta"); if (meta) meta.textContent = date + (rev.change_summary ? Do not invent content."; var aiConfig = collectAISettingsFormData(); var provider = aiConfig.provider || "openrouter"; var model = getSelectedAIModel(aiConfig) || "gpt-4o"; var messages = [ { role: "system", content: systemPrompt }, { role: "user", content: "Extract from this brainstorm dump:\n\n" + source.content } ]; callEditorAI({ provider: provider, model: model, messages: messages, temperature: 0.3, max_tokens: 2000 }).then(function (result) { var text = result && result.text ? result.text.trim() : ""; if (!text) throw new Error("AI returned empty response."); // Try to parse JSON from the response var jsonMatch = text.match(/\[[\s\S]*\]/); if (!jsonMatch) throw new Error("AI did not return valid JSON array."); var extracted = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]); if (!Array.isArray(extracted) || !extracted.length) throw new Error("No extractions found."); // Create proposals for each extraction var proposalPromises = extracted.map(function (item) { var pillarKey = item.pillar_key || ""; var content = item.content || ""; if (!pillarKey || !content) return Promise.resolve(null); var pillarDef = WORLD_FRAMEWORK_PILLARS.find(function (p) { return p.key === pillarKey; }); return saveWorldProposal(seriesId, { proposal_type: "add_framework_statement", summary: pillarDef ? pillarDef.label + ": " + content.substring(0, 80) : content.substring(0, 100), proposed_content: { text: content, pillar_key: pillarKey, source: "brainstorm", source_id: source.id }, source_passage: content.substring(0, 500), source_pillar_key: pillarKey, review_status: "pending" }); }); return Promise.all(proposalPromises.filter(Boolean)); }).then(function (results) { if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = "Extract"; } if (item) item.classList.remove("is-extracting"); var count = results ? results.filter(Boolean).length : 0; if (count) { setStatus("Extracted " + count + " elements. Review them in the Review tab.", "ok"); // Switch to Review mode to show the proposals switchCodexMode("review"); var project = getSelectedProject(); var seriesId = project ? Number(project.series_id || 0) : 0; if (seriesId) loadReviewProposals(seriesId); } else { setStatus("No extractable elements found.", "warn"); } }).catch(function (err) { if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = "Extract"; } if (item) item.classList.remove("is-extracting"); setStatus("Extraction failed: " + (err.message || err), "error"); }); } var _editingBrainstormSourceId = 0; function openBrainstormSourceModal(source) { var modal = document.getElementById("brainstormSourceModal"); if (!modal) return; _editingBrainstormSourceId = source ? (source.content || "") : ""; var fileInput = document.getElementById("brainstormSourceFileInput"); if (fileInput) fileInput.value = ""; var modalTitle = modal.querySelector(".modal-header h3"); if (modalTitle) modalTitle.textContent = source ? Try a text file instead.", "error"); return; } pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = "https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js"; pdfjsLib.getDocument(typedarray).promise.then(function (pdf) { var textParts = []; var pagePromises = []; for (var i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) { pagePromises.push(pdf.getPage(i).then(function (page) { return page.getTextContent().then(function (textContent) { return textContent.items.map(function (item) { return item.str; }).join(" "); }); })); } return Promise.all(pagePromises); }).then(function (pageTexts) { var fullText = pageTexts.join("\n\n"); var contentInput = document.getElementById("brainstormSourceContent"); if (contentInput) contentInput.value = fullText; }).catch(function (err) { setStatus("PDF extraction failed: " + (err.message || err), "error"); }); }; reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file); } else { // Text file - read as text var reader = new FileReader(); reader.onload = function (e) { var text = e.target ? e.target.result : ""; var contentInput = document.getElementById("brainstormSourceContent"); if (contentInput) contentInput.value = text; }; reader.readAsText(file); } }); } var saveBtn = document.getElementById("brainstormSourceSaveBtn"); if (saveBtn && !saveBtn._wired) { saveBtn._wired = true; saveBtn.addEventListener("click", function () { var project = getSelectedProject(); var seriesId = project ? }); renderAssistantMessages(); renderSessionSelector(); }); } function getRelatedCodexEntities(pillar) { var seriesId = skCurrentSeriesId || (getSelectedProject() && Number(getSelectedProject().series_id || 0)); var score = 0; if (content.indexOf(name) !== -1) score += 3; if (name.indexOf(content.substring(0, 20)) !== -1) score += 2; if (desc && content.indexOf(desc.substring(0, 50)) !== -1) score += 1; // Check word overlap var contentWords = content.split(/\s+/).filter(function (w) { return w.length > 3; }); var entityText = (name + " " + desc).toLowerCase(); contentWords.forEach(function (w) { if (entityText.indexOf(w) !== -1) score += 0.5; }); return { entity: e, score: score }; }).filter(function (s) { return s.score > 0; }); scored.sort(function (a, b) { return b.score - a.score; }); return scored.slice(0, 8).map(function (s) { var typeKey = (s.entity.codex_entity_types || {}).key || ""; return { name: s.entity.name || "Untitled", type: typeKey, summary: s.entity.summary || "", description: s.entity.description || "" }; "; }); html += "
"; container.innerHTML = html; // Wire action buttons container.querySelectorAll(".proposal-accept-btn").forEach(function (btn) { btn.addEventListener("click", function () { var proposalId = Number(btn.getAttribute("data-proposal-id")); var action = btn.getAttribute("data-action"); handleProposalAction(proposalId, action); }); }); container.querySelectorAll(".proposal-reject-btn").forEach(function (btn) { btn.addEventListener("click", function () { var proposalId = Number(btn.getAttribute("data-proposal-id")); var reason = prompt("Rejection reason (optional):", ""); if (reason === null) return; handleProposalAction(proposalId, "rejected", reason); }); }); container.querySelectorAll(".proposal-archive-btn").forEach(function (btn) { btn.addEventListener("click", function () { var proposalId = Number(btn.getAttribute("data-proposal-id")); handleProposalAction(proposalId, "archived"); }); }); // Wire bulk actions var acceptAllBtn = document.getElementById("proposalAcceptAllBtn"); if (acceptAllBtn) { acceptAllBtn.addEventListener("click", function () { if (!confirm("Accept all " + _reviewProposals.length + " proposals?")) return; var project = getSelectedProject(); var seriesId = project ? Number(project.series_id || 0) : 0; if (!seriesId) return; var proposalsToProcess = _reviewProposals.slice(); acceptAllBtn.disabled = true; acceptAllBtn.textContent = "Accepting..."; var chain = Promise.resolve(); proposalsToProcess.forEach(function (proposal) { chain = chain.then(function () { return handleProposalActionAsync(proposal.id, "accepted_canon"); }); }); chain.then(function () { acceptAllBtn.disabled = false; acceptAllBtn.textContent = "Accept All"; setStatus("All proposals accepted.", "ok"); _reviewProposals = []; renderProposalList(); }); }); } var rejectAllBtn = document.getElementById("proposalRejectAllBtn"); if (rejectAllBtn) { rejectAllBtn.addEventListener("click", function () { if (!confirm("Reject all " + _reviewProposals.length + " proposals?")) return; var proposalsToProcess = _reviewProposals.slice(); rejectAllBtn.disabled = true; rejectAllBtn.textContent = "Rejecting..."; var chain = Promise.resolve(); proposalsToProcess.forEach(function (proposal) { chain = chain.then(function () { return updateWorldProposalStatus(proposal.id, "rejected", "Bulk rejected"); }); }); chain.then(function () { rejectAllBtn.disabled = false; rejectAllBtn.textContent = "Reject All"; setStatus("All proposals rejected.", "ok"); _reviewProposals = []; renderProposalList(); }); }); } } function handleProposalActionAsync(proposalId, action, reason) { var proposal = _reviewProposals.find(function (p) { return Number(p.id) === proposalId; }); if (!proposal) return Promise.resolve(); var project = getSelectedProject(); var seriesId = project ? Number(project.series_id || 0) : 0; if (!seriesId) return Promise.resolve(); // For add_framework_statement, append to pillar if (proposal.proposal_type === "add_framework_statement") { var pillarKey = proposal.source_pillar_key || ""; var pillar = _worldPillars.find(function (p) { return p.pillar_key === pillarKey; }); if (pillar && _worldFramework) { var newContent = pillar.author_content ? pillar.author_content + "\n\n" + (proposal.proposed_content.text || "") : (proposal.proposed_content.text || ""); pillar.author_content = newContent; pillar.state = "captured"; return saveWorldPillar(_worldFramework.id, pillarKey, pillar).then(function () { return updateWorldProposalStatus(proposalId, action, reason); }).then(function () { _reviewProposals = _reviewProposals.filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) !== proposalId; }); renderPillarGrid(); }); } } // For create_entity, create the entity if (proposal.proposal_type === "create_entity") { var content = proposal.proposed_content || {}; var entityTypeKey = proposal.target_entity_type || "lore"; var entityTypeId = getCodexEntityTypeId_(seriesId, entityTypeKey); if (!entityTypeId) return Promise.resolve(); var entityPayload = { name: content.name || proposal.summary || "Untitled", description: content.description || "", summary: content.summary || "", scope: "series", status: "active", canon_status: action === "accepted_canon" ? "canon" : "provisional", custom_data: content.custom_data || {} }; return callAppsScriptJson("saveCodexEntity", [seriesId, entityTypeId, JSON.stringify(entityPayload)]).then(function (result) { var saved = result && result.entity ? result.entity : {}; var savedId = Number(saved.id || 0); if (savedId) return linkProposalToEntity(proposalId, savedId); }).then(function () { return updateWorldProposalStatus(proposalId, action, reason); }).then(function () { _reviewProposals = _reviewProposals.filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) !== proposalId; }); return loadCodexEntities(seriesId); }); } // Default: just update status return updateWorldProposalStatus(proposalId, action, reason).then(function () { _reviewProposals = _reviewProposals.filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) !== proposalId; }); }); } function handleProposalAction(proposalId, action, reason) { var proposal = _reviewProposals.find(function (p) { return Number(p.id) === proposalId; }); if (!proposal) return; var project = getSelectedProject(); var seriesId = project ? Number(project.series_id || 0) : 0; if (!seriesId) return; var newStatus = action === "accept" ? "accepted_canon" : action === "accept_provisional" ? "accepted_provisional" : action; // If accepting a create_entity proposal, actually create the entity if ((newStatus === "accepted_canon" || newStatus === "accepted_provisional") && proposal.proposal_type === "create_entity") { var content = proposal.proposed_content || {}; var entityTypeKey = proposal.target_entity_type || "lore"; var entityTypeId = getCodexEntityTypeId_(seriesId, entityTypeKey); if (!entityTypeId) { setStatus("Unknown entity type: " + entityTypeKey, "error"); return; } var entityPayload = { name: content.name || proposal.summary || "Untitled", description: content.description || "", summary: content.summary || "", scope: "series", status: "active", canon_status: newStatus === "accepted_canon" ? "canon" : "provisional", custom_data: content.custom_data || {} }; callAppsScriptJson("saveCodexEntity", [seriesId, entityTypeId, JSON.stringify(entityPayload)]).then(function (result) { var saved = result && result.entity ? result.entity : {}; var savedId = Number(saved.id || 0); if (savedId) { return linkProposalToEntity(proposalId, savedId).then(function () { return updateWorldProposalStatus(proposalId, newStatus, reason); }); } return updateWorldProposalStatus(proposalId, newStatus, reason); }).then(function () { setStatus("Proposal accepted: " + proposal.summary, "ok"); _reviewProposals = _reviewProposals.filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) !== proposalId; }); renderProposalList(); return loadCodexEntities(seriesId); }).then(function () { renderCodexTree(seriesId); }); } else if ((newStatus === "accepted_canon" || newStatus === "accepted_provisional") && proposal.proposal_type === "add_framework_statement") { // Add content to the relevant pillar var pillarKey = proposal.source_pillar_key || ""; var pillar = _worldPillars.find(function (p) { return p.pillar_key === pillarKey; }); if (pillar && _worldFramework) { var newContent = pillar.author_content ? pillar.author_content + "\n\n" + (proposal.proposed_content.text || "") : (proposal.proposed_content.text || ""); pillar.author_content = newContent; pillar.state = "captured"; saveWorldPillar(_worldFramework.id, pillarKey, pillar).then(function () { return updateWorldProposalStatus(proposalId, newStatus, reason); }).then(function () { setStatus("Framework statement accepted: " + proposal.summary, "ok"); _reviewProposals = _reviewProposals.filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) !== proposalId; }); renderProposalList(); renderPillarGrid(); }); } else { updateWorldProposalStatus(proposalId, newStatus, reason).then(function () { _reviewProposals = _reviewProposals.filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) !== proposalId; }); renderProposalList(); }); } } else { // Just update status (reject, archive) updateWorldProposalStatus(proposalId, newStatus, reason).then(function () { setStatus("Proposal " + newStatus + ": " + proposal.summary, "ok"); _reviewProposals = _reviewProposals.filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) !== proposalId; }); renderProposalList(); }); } } // ── AI Prompt Editor ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── var STORAGE_KEY_AI_PROMPTS = "writing_app_ai_prompts"; var _aiPromptsCache = null; // cached from Supabase function loadCustomAiPrompts() { // Prefer Supabase cache, fall back to localStorage if (_aiPromptsCache) return _aiPromptsCache; try { var raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY_AI_PROMPTS); return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null; } catch (e) { return null; } } function saveCustomAiPrompts(prompts) { _aiPromptsCache = prompts; try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_AI_PROMPTS, JSON.stringify(prompts)); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } // Save to Supabase in background if (typeof supabaseRunner !== "undefined") { supabaseRunner .withSuccessHandler(function () {}) .withFailureHandler(function (err) { console.warn("AI prompts Supabase save failed:", err); }) .saveSupabaseAiPromptsJsonFromClient(prompts || {}); } } function loadAiPromptsFromSupabase() { if (typeof supabaseRunner === "undefined") return Promise.resolve(); return new Promise(function (resolve) { supabaseRunner .withSuccessHandler(function (json) { try { var result = JSON.parse(json); if (result.ok && result.prompts && Object.keys(result.prompts).length > 0) { _aiPromptsCache = result.prompts; try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_AI_PROMPTS, JSON.stringify(result.prompts)); } catch (e) {} } } catch (e) {} resolve(); }) .withFailureHandler(function () { resolve(); }) .getSupabaseAiPromptsJsonFromClient(); }); } function getWorldBuilderPrompts() { var custom = loadCustomAiPrompts(); var wb = custom && custom.world_builder ? custom.world_builder : null; }); if (wb.system_template) base.system_template = wb.system_template; if (wb.pillar_help) base.pillar_help = wb.pillar_help; return base; } function deepMerge(target, source) { Object.keys(source).forEach(function (k) { if (source[k] && typeof source[k] === "object" && !Array.isArray(source[k])) { if (!target[k]) target[k] = {}; deepMerge(target[k], source[k]); } else { target[k] = source[k]; } }); } function getCustomAiPromptsAll() { var custom = loadCustomAiPrompts() || {}; var base = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_AI_PROMPTS)); "; // Load state records supabaseRunner .withSuccessHandler(function (json) { try { var result = JSON.parse(json); if (!result.ok || !result.states.length) { if (stateList) stateList.innerHTML = "

"; } else { var html = ""; // Build book lookup map var bookMap = {}; if (typeof appState !== "undefined" && Array.isArray(appState.projects)) { appState.projects.forEach(function (p) { bookMap[Number(p.project_id || p.id || 0)] = p; }); } // Preload chapters for all books that have state records var bookIds = {}; result.states.forEach(function (s) { if (s.book_id) bookIds[Number(s.book_id)] = true; }); var chapterPromises = Object.keys(bookIds).map(function (bid) { return codexV2LoadChapters(Number(bid)); }); Promise.all(chapterPromises).then(function () { // Sort by effective_from_sort ascending (narrative order), then by property name, then entry_order // Fallback: group by book_id, chapter_id when effective_from_sort is null var sortedStates = result.states.slice().sort(function (a, b) { var sortA = a.effective_from_sort != null ? Number(a.effective_from_sort) : null; var sortB = b.effective_from_sort != null ? Number(b.effective_from_sort) : null; // If both have effective_from_sort, compare directly if (sortA != null && sortB != null) { if (sortA !== sortB) return sortA - sortB; } // If only one has it, it comes first if (sortA != null && sortB == null) return -1; if (sortA == null && sortB != null) return 1; // Both null: sort by book_id, then chapter_id var bookA = Number(a.book_id || 0); var bookB = Number(b.book_id || 0); if (bookA !== bookB) return bookA - bookB; var chA = Number(a.chapter_id || 0); var chB = Number(b.chapter_id || 0); if (chA !== chB) return chA - chB; // Same position: sort by property name, then entry_order var nameA = (a.property_key || "").toLowerCase(); var nameB = (b.property_key || "").toLowerCase(); if (nameA !== nameB) return nameA.localeCompare(nameB); var orderA = a.entry_order != null ? Number(a.entry_order) : 1; var orderB = b.entry_order != null ? Number(b.entry_order) : 1; return orderA - orderB; }); var lastBookId = null; var lastChapterId = null; sortedStates.forEach(function (s) { // Add chapter section header when book/chapter changes var sBookId = Number(s.book_id || 0); var sChapterId = Number(s.chapter_id || 0); if (sBookId !== lastBookId || sChapterId !== lastChapterId) { lastBookId = sBookId; lastChapterId = sChapterId; var sectionLabel = ""; if (sBookId) { var book = bookMap[sBookId]; sectionLabel = book ? book.title : "Book #" + sBookId; if (sChapterId) { var chapters = (typeof skCache !== "undefined" && skCache.chapters && skCache.chapters[sBookId]) ? skCache.chapters[sBookId] : []; var chapterLabel = typeof codexV2GetChapterLabel === "function" ? codexV2GetChapterLabel(chapters, sChapterId) : "Ch." + sChapterId; sectionLabel += " — " + chapterLabel; } } else { sectionLabel = "No book/chapter"; } html += "
" + escapeHtml(sectionLabel) + "
"; } var value = s.value_number != null ? s.value_number : s.value_text || (s.value_json ? JSON.stringify(s.value_json) : "—"); html += "
"; html += "
"; html += "" + escapeHtml(s.property_key || "property") + ""; if (s.entry_order != null && Number(s.entry_order) > 1) { html += " #" + s.entry_order + ""; } html += " = " + escapeHtml(String(value)) + ""; if (s.state_status) html += " [" + escapeHtml(s.state_status) + "]"; if (s.notes) html += " — " + escapeHtml(s.notes) + ""; html += "
"; html += ""; html += ""; html += "
"; }); if (stateList) stateList.innerHTML = html; // Wire edit/delete buttons if (stateList) { stateList.querySelectorAll(".codex-state-edit").forEach(function (btn) { btn.addEventListener("click", function () { var stateId = Number(btn.getAttribute("data-state-id")); var state = result.states.find(function (s) { return Number(s.id) === stateId; }); if (state) showStateEditForm(seriesId, entityId, state); }); }); stateList.querySelectorAll(".codex-state-delete").forEach(function (btn) { btn.addEventListener("click", function () { var stateId = Number(btn.getAttribute("data-state-id")); if (confirm("Delete this state record?")) { supabaseRunner .withSuccessHandler(function () { loadCodexStateAndEvents(seriesId, entityId); }) .withFailureHandler(function (err) { console.error("[codexV2] Delete state failed:", err); }) .deleteCodexEntityState(stateId); } }); }); } }); // end Promise.all.then } } catch (e) { if (stateList) stateList.innerHTML = "

Number(existingVal.id) : 0; var payload = { entity_id: entityId, field_definition_id: def.id, value_text: null, value_number: null, value_boolean: null, value_json: null, canon_status: "draft" }; if (fieldType === "boolean") { payload.value_boolean = input.checked; } else if (fieldType === "number") { payload.value_number = input.value ? Number(input.value) : null; } else if (fieldType === "entity_link") { var linkedId = input.value ? Number(input.value) : null; payload.linked_entity_id = linkedId; payload.value_text = linkedId && input.selectedIndex >= 0 ? input.options[input.selectedIndex].textContent : null; } else { payload.value_text = input.value || null; } if (valueId) payload.id = valueId; promises.push(new Promise(function (resolve) { supabaseRunner .withSuccessHandler(function () { resolve(true); }) .withFailureHandler(function (err) { console.warn("[codexV2] Failed to save field:", def.field_key, err); resolve(false); }) .saveCodexEntityFieldValue(JSON.stringify(payload)); })); }); Promise.all(promises).then(function (results) { var successCount = results.filter(function (r) { return r; }).length; if (saveBtn) { saveBtn.textContent = "Saved (" + successCount + ")"; saveBtn.disabled = false; } setTimeout(function () { if (saveBtn) saveBtn.textContent = "Save Fields"; }, 2000); }); } // ============================================================ // PROGRESSION SHEET — Read-only character state at a narrative point // ============================================================ function loadProgressionSheet(seriesId, entityId) { var container = document.getElementById("codexProgressionSheet"); if (!container) return; var html = ""; // Narrative point selector html += "
